## Notes for the C12 audit

- Region names and the access kinds are produced by MemberTrace: peersA/maskA/playersA (session object
  session 0 offset 0x60..0x226C) and headerB/peersB/maskB/playersB (0x226C..0x4538).
- `0x44FF4D|peersA` is the hot accessor (tens of thousands of hits per dump); `0x44EA95..0x44EADD`
  walk `playersA` fields in 0x10 steps (0x1780-0x17D0).
- Hit counts are cumulative per distinct instruction between dumps; the dumps repeat every 30 s, so the
  same instruction appears once per dump with its running count.
